route_consumption_estimator.plugins.vehicle_information.epa_database_parser_scripts.parse_vehicles_xlsx_sql 

 1import pandas as pd
 2
 3from route_consumption_estimator.plugins.vehicle_information.epa_database_parser_scripts.constants import *
 4
 5# Load the Excel file
 6df = pd.read_excel('vehicles_data/parsed_vehicles.xlsx')
 7
 8vehicle_info = "('{name}', '{engine}', {mass}, {pmaxkw}, {conversion}, {factor}, {a}, {b}, {c}, '', ''),\n"
 9
10sql_commands = """
11
12USE greta_app;
13
14INSERT INTO vehicle (Name, MotorType, UnladenVehMass, PMaxKw, LitersConversion, ResistanceFactor, A, B, C, Url, ImageUrl)
15VALUES 
16"""
17
18for index, row in df.iterrows():
19    name = row['Represented Test Veh Make'] + '_-_' + row['Represented Test Veh Model']
20    engine = str(row['Test Fuel Type Description']).upper()
21
22    if engine == 'DIESEL':
23        conversion = DEFAULT_DIESEL_CONVERSION
24    elif engine == 'GASOLINE':
25        conversion = DEFAULT_GASOLINE_CONVERSION
26    else:
27        conversion = -1.0
28
29    mass = row['Equivalent Test Weight (kg)']
30    pmaxkw = row['Rated Power (kW)']
31    a = row['Target Coef A (N)']
32    b = row['Target Coef B (N/kph)']
33    c = row['Target Coef C (N/kph**2)']
34    # Factor now is by default as is it not specified
35    factor = DEFAULT_VEHICLE_RF
36    sql_commands += vehicle_info.format(name=name, engine=engine, mass=mass, pmaxkw=pmaxkw, conversion=conversion,
37                                        factor=factor, a=a, b=b, c=c)
38
39# Remove the last ',' and add ;
40sql_commands = sql_commands[:-2] + ';'
41
42# Write the SQL commands to a file
43with open('vehicles_data/vehicles_db.sql', 'w') as f:
44    f.write(sql_commands)
